available for brand and lifestyle products. These will probably also be taxed heavier.
Sharing will become more important because in the short term this provides the best
contribution to the reduction of the use of raw materials and energy. In a circular
and sharing economy, Blockchain Organizing will play an important role. Our lives
and survival as a specie is especially threatened by our current system based on continuous economic growth. We must grow two percent per year to repay debts that
we have created to make grow possible. Economics growth is inherent to the system.
But if you continue this, our children must consume eighty percent more and our
grandchildren three hundred percent. That is impossible on a finite planet.
Time for blockchain pioneers
Blockchain Organizing mainly needs pioneers. A pioneer is an entrepreneur who is
one of the first to enter a certain domain, and has to find his way in there. Plus, a
pioneer cannot use the experience of another person who has ever been there. Along
the way there are no tools to be found, this must be considered before the pioneer
starts the expedition. A pioneer has vision, whereby an effective vision is a sustainable imagination that has the power to become reality. Characteristic of pioneering
are the setbacks one experiences, the hardships one undergoes, the delayed desire,
the success that one must deny oneself and the large effort that one has to make
because all kinds of facilities are still missing. The work of a pioneer is often accompanied by many failures, but the pioneer learns from that. A pioneer does not have
methods that have been proved in practice. A pioneer must have courage and be
prepared to make a journey nobody else has made. Courage is not the absence of
fear, but the notion that something is more important in your life than you. Or as
the American writer Meg Cabot writes193: “Courage is not the absence of fear, but rather the
judgment that something else is more important than fear. The brave may not live forever, but the
cautious do not live at all. From now on you'll be traveling the road between who you think you are
and who you can be. The key is to allow yourself to make the journey.”
A pioneer knows that a lot of people are afraid, not knowing where society will go.
But this vacuum gives space for hope. It gives space for letting go the old and a time
193 Originally by: Ambrose Hollingworth Redmoon, pseudonym for the writer James Holling-

worth (1933-1996).

216

for new thinking and doing. The work of a pioneer is very important, so other can
build on it. A pioneer looks at a problem in such a way that the solution for the
problem is evident. In this dynamic and hectic time, pioneering has the character of
admitting color: show who you are and what you stand for. A pioneer shows and
experiences inspiration in the work performed and how it is done. A pioneer is able
to give substance to developments that are necessary and important in the current
world. A pioneer dares to let go of old securities in order to create space to look from
a new perspective. In doing so, rely on abundance, in spite of those who regard scarcity as a fact. A pioneer dares to rely on imagination, in spite of those who feel captured by existing conditioning. A pioneer is able to let go of comfortable ideas because these do not challenge the way of thinking. Being able to offer genuine value
by serving the best to a next development stage in humanity is characteristic for a
pioneer. The road to more maturity is cleared. A road more and more people are
ready and longing for. A pioneer is deeply convinced that we humans are capable of
much more than what we now deliver. A pioneer knows that not experiencing the
results of his effort is no reason not to make the effort.
Is it time for a new Enlightenment?
The question is: will man, will democracy survive, will or prosperity sustain in a rapidly globalizing, digitizing and centralizing society when we continue to use old forms
to organize supply and demand? We believe it is time for a new form of Enlightenment with which we can separate ourselves, as human beings, from existing conditions, institutions and management paradigms. For example, in the past we have separated ourselves from dogmas of ‘the church’, the State and other self-evident things
like exclusion of women and minorities. Now it is time that we separate ourselves
from organization paradigms and tools which are more and more of a burden to us
than a pleasure. We mean tools that we use, but that are not transparent, that we
cannot control and for which there are actually no alternatives. Tools that, step by
step, without being aware of it and perhaps not so intended, take away our human
freedom and keep us more trapped. One of the best-known Enlighteners is Immanuel Kant, whose offers the following insight: “Enlightenment is man’s emergence from his
self-imposed immaturity. Immaturity is the inability to use one’s understanding without guidance
from another. 194”
His insight is still relevant. Immanuel Kant bases himself on the autonomous person,
who will not be led by others. ‘Dare to think’ he says. With this he responds to people
who state: ‘I cannot help it because I was born this way’. According to Kant, this
means that you do not even accept your own dignity. Many people cover themselves
in advance, also with regard to using traditional management concepts like: ‘It is
never my problem, but always the problem of the other person’. By saying or seeing
this, people make themselves incapable of action and thus inferior, not compared to
other people, but more and more often compared to the tools they use. Besides ‘the
other person’, the circumstances are usually blamed. Never myself. But
194 For ‘Guidance from another’, you can also read ‘help from a certain tool’.

Boek8_Kopie_Intern_Gebruik_Weconomics

217

circumstances and tools cannot choose, only people can choose. You cannot blame
the actual circumstances or tools, of the world you want to live in. You cannot blame
the rain that you have forgotten your umbrella. The right to individual self-development can increasingly only be exercised by people who also have access to the data
that is known about them. We mean ‘data self-determination’ and ‘data self-sovereignty’. ‘Be a person’, says Kant and carry your responsibility. The situation that we
can think, be aware and relate to, what affects us, makes us human and distinguishes
us from other animal species and tools. We will desperately need this feature with all
technological violence that comes our way (unless we choose differently) such as195:
Artificial Intelligence, Machine Learning, Robotics and Internet of Things. A democracy can only survive, if the decisions that we take as a person, are based on data that
people can also acquire in freedom and is not pre-sorted by a tool. When we cannot
make a choice in freedom, it undermines our constitution, our community and our
fundamental human rights. We are clearly at a crossroad in our existence. If we take
the wrong decision now, it can have serious consequences for the future of our children. Anyway, Blockchain Organizing offers a way for people to ‘regain control’ of
their own data and the tools that they use to transfer data from seller to buyer.
Homo prospectus
People will try to keep what they have got. That is not a status quo, but progress.
Less growth and more control does not mean that we take a step back in our evolution, but rather a step forward. From uncontrolled welfare growth, to sustainable
welfare, wellbeing and more surplus time is also growth. It means that we replace a
system that is threatening to become unstable. Evolution is not always quicker, bigger
or more. Evolution is mainly adapting when the circumstances require this, so a better chance of survival is created. More and more people will enter the highest development stage (holistically), known to us and be aware of our relationship with the
total. This will create a less complex society. A society that is much more based on
man and less on what man has created. A society with a human dimension where
common sense and virtue beat the rules, where values beat procedures and where
people are again more important than tools.
That may sound contradictory coming from two blockchain evangelists, but we hope
that this book has made it clear what we stand for: we not only see blockchain technology as a tool to solve management problems, but also social problems. Organizing
trust plays an important role in this. And Blockchain Organizing main characteristic
is probably that it can organize trust, without increasing complexity and therefor distrust. If that is possible, we may have made an important 'discovery' and reinvented
management.
When we discover this, we will start solving problems that really matter. The way we
currently look at problems, is the problem. We, as humans, we are a kind of problem195 Cybernetics is the bases of many of our new technologies. Cybernetics is derived from the

Greek 'helmsman'. It is the science that deals with the management of natural organization and
mechanical systems that learn with the help of feedback (loops).

Boek8_Kopie_Intern_Gebruik_Weconomics

218

solving super organism196. Not so much due to our individual intelligence but due to
the fact that we as human beings are able to cooperate and image something that is
not there yet. The Homo Sapiens, is able to image the solution of a problem. Therefore, the name Homo Sapiens is probably less suitable. We are defined as wise people, but we are mainly the Homo Prospectus: the foresighted man. To understand
this, the insights from the book ‘Homo Prospectus197’ will help. This book is about
positive psychology. Evolution has developed some sort of brain in man that mainly
focuses on ‘being able to think ahead’. As man we first ‘started’ a social-cultural and
later a technical evolution line. Our brain is able to anticipate and to visualize. We
are able to come up with ideas, these ideas are our future and we can transfer these
to others. Unfortunately, we are less able to stop bad ideas on time. Certain forms of
capitalism, such as the ideas from Ayn Rand198, come close to this last phenomenon.
Former director of the New York University, John Sawhill, formulated it as follows:
“A society is defined not only by what it creates, but by what it refuses to destroy.”
Our brains try to understand the past and the present, to be able to choose actions
that lead to progress and a better future for our children. This ‘thinking ahead’ provides us with an enormous advantage compared to other animal species. The possibility to be able to ‘see’ and evaluate future actions gives us an enormous advantage.
Returning to the past, a past that we have left for good reasons, will therefore not
help destructive populists in the long term. We ‘do not look back’, we ‘look ahead’.
Much existing literature looks at the past as predictor of the future. According to us,
and the authors of the book Homo Prospectus, this is only partially true. Looking
ahead is using learned experiences from the past, but also from our capacity to image
solutions. In this book we discussed the negative influence of old conditionings when
forming solutions for social problems. We must isolate and weaken old ideas and
concepts that no longer work and strengthen new ideas, which are expected to work
(according to imagination). As said before: Blockchain Organizing refuses to be what
it is. It is mainly a concept what can make you think and help you with the reinvention
of management. It would already be a great start when Blockchain Organizing is an
idea for you with which you can experiment without immediately having to accept
the idea. We like to hear your views on Blockchain Organizing and what you can and
cannot do with it. We like to finish with a nice insight of Aristoteles:
“It is the mark of an educated mind to be able to entertain a thought without accepting it.”

About the authors
Paul Bessems is an international speaker, blockchain consultant and (co) author of
more than ten management books. He has more than 25 years experience in designing and developing new organizations that align better with our new context (data
economy) and new organization technologies such as blockchain. He is an expert in
the field of Blockchain Organizing. He helps organizations with the transition ‘From
Business to Networkorganization’ and answering questions like: ‘What is Blockchain Organizing, why is it important, how can I use it (and how not) and how should we start with it?’
Besides his work as a consultant, he is also chairman and fellow of the Weconomics
Foundation. Weconomics is a network that facilitates the transition to a more sustainable prosperity. The core in this development is the deployment of organization
technology such as blockchain to create surplus for a new ‘work sphere’.
Paul is also founder and CEO of Weconet Blockchain Technologies, located in Eindhoven, The Netherlands, one of the smartest regions in the world. Weconet helps
organization to use new technologies such as blockchain and align these new technologies in new organization models such as shared transaction networks. Paul is
also founder and fellow of The Institute for New Organizational Thinking.
Paul studied industrial engineering and management science at the Technical University in Eindhoven. In 1992 he introduced the Harvard Case Method in the Netherlands, developed several master programs for universities and developed two
PHD’s research proposals. In 1992 he started Buro Staff Support, his first professional community. Since then he helped dozens of professional communities. In recent years, these communities more and more work with the concept of Blockchain
Organizing.
Walter Bril is a process knowledge management expert. For more than 25 years now
he has been involved in analyzing, (re) designing business processes, with his focus
mainly in the field of practical knowledge retention management. Currently this
shows concretely in the form of contributing to the, San Francisco based, SaaS startup Elements.cloud. As one of the original co-creators of the Universal Process Notation (UPN) and member of the core team of Elements.cloud, he adds value to
mainly Salesforce customers.
In addition, somewhere in 2014 he started to discover and study the interesting developments around blockchain and joined one of the first blockchain registered certification MOOCS. Particularly because his concerns related to the sustainability of
traditional business models and the potential socio-economic opportunities that
blockchain has to offer, he has turned into a fervent blockchain evangelist. The combination technologist, process knowledge 'nerd' and blockchain evangelist turned out
to be particularly valuable!

220

Walter studied business informatics at the Rijkshogeschool IJsselland in Deventer
the Netherlands, has a semi-technical background, is author of several blogs, management books and international trainer and speaker. Besides that he actually does
have a social life and loves to perform music, read, enjoy running and traveling.
Weconet Technologies
Both authors work for Weconet Technologies BV, a spin-off from the Weconomics
Foundation. Weconet brings information technology and organizational science together to solve social problems. Weconet Technologies was founded in one of the
world smartest regions: Brainport Eindhoven in the Netherlands. We see blockchain
technology not just as technology, but as a form of organization technology (#orgtech) that enables us to manage transactions and organizations decentralized, autonomous and almost without any friction between supply and demand.
The right deployment of Blockchain Organizing means that your organization will
be more productive and flexible, that you comply with the new privacy law (GDPR),
will be less depending on big data- and software companies and give a new meaning
to human work. For more information about our solutions go to
www.weconet.org/solutions
For more background information with this book, including a literature list and
blockchain knowledge center, see also the website: weconomics.org (Dutch) and
weconet.org (English).
